================================================================================
 COMPREHENSIVE COMPATIBILITY & DIAGNOSTIC REPORT FOR 1,440+ COMIC SOURCES
 Generated at: 2026-10-02T14:55:25.927Z
 Total Sources Cataloged: 1442
================================================================================

EXPLANATION OF IMPORT BEHAVIOR FOR CATALOG SOURCES:
1. Catalog Base URLs (Homepages / Directory Indexes):
   - Most entries in the Keiyoushi/Tachiyomi extension catalog list the ROOT base URL (e.g., https://asuracomic.net) rather than an individual chapter reading link.
   - Attempting to scrape a root homepage directly returns 0 comic panel images because homepages contain featured carousels, lists, and banners instead of a chapter reader area.
   - SOLUTION: Users must import specific chapter reading URLs (e.g., https://asuracomic.net/series/.../chapter-1) which successfully activate the MangaThemesia, Madara, Webtoons, Paginated, or TS-Main-Image engines.

2. Cloudflare & Anti-Bot Protection (403 / 503 / Challenge):
   - High-traffic scanlation sites use Cloudflare Browser Defense or DDoS protection, blocking direct HTTP fetch probes without valid session cookies or bypassing proxies.

3. Domain Rotations & Dead Links:
   - Scanlation websites frequently change domains (e.g., .com -> .net -> .xyz) due to copyright takedowns or hosting migrations, resulting in DNS resolution failures or HTTP 404s.
